## Production rules

- MATCH GMR’s contiguous verbatim English from the same source; never compose it or modify/promote hgm_gloss.
- Preserve PROVISIONAL/TENTATIVE labels. Machine semantic review is not human acceptance.
- Keep independent proposal angles, reconciliation and skeptical semantic review. Current Codex handover replaces historical model-role names with available Codex agents; record actual provenance, never impersonate an old Claude model or call paid APIs.
- Use d5 for words/established compounds, d7 for unique tighter members immediately after their d5 parent. Recast phrases stay at phrase depth only when the complete correspondence is defensible.
- Null means no separately alignable English exponent. Proven grammar fused into another English word may be null with an explicit fusion note; it is not absent meaning. Unresolved or contradictory mappings are omitted. Distributed content stays unwrapped or in a defensible whole phrase; higher depth does not excuse false correspondence.
- C05:177’s full definite/indefinite phrase is approved contextually because the elided English predicate can include definitely and 178 resumes the definite eleven. Keep its explicit ambiguity note; never turn it into ma nges → not experienced.
- Preserve source SNYING RDZE/RDZES: it is glossary-attested compassion. Do not revive the refuted 183 correction or force DOD/driving; the unresolved rescue construction remains unpaired. The older auto-aligned bsdums gloss is a conflicting reference; current agreement evidence does not authorize changing the master.
- C13 stays blocked for alignment until upstream repair. No new C13 scan or offset detector. A metadata-only check found zero installed-pack records in the known C13:63–82 window; that is not a new corpus audit.
- No messages from user accounts, paid generation, purchases, public publishing, or history rewrites. The separate composed AI-glossary lane remains parked.

## Recovered work and generation

101 historical scratch files were recovered with source/copy SHA-256 agreement.
`handoff-check/recovered-c05/b19/` has both angles through 172 and historical
reconciliations 166–169; `b20/` has both angles 173–175. These have now served the
landed pages through 175. Future segments need new independent proposals.

`campaign-artifacts/land_reviewed_page.py` materializes a manually approved
three-segment page, preserves accepted specs/reviews and connects prior-page
navigation. It does NOT approve, register, rebuild or run landing gates. Read the
semantic verdict first. The canonical body is unchanged; new-page credits say
Codex and the inherited missing depth 7 label is defined. Update the registry and
C05 index after each page, rebuild both banks and dictionary exports, run the
seventeen targeted checks (the prior sixteen plus `dictionary_exports`), verify
retained data and actual new counts, append ledger and commit. After each landing,
also run `campaign-artifacts/verify_course_csv.py BASE_COMMIT REPORT_PATH` to
compare every course/depth row and metadata field with the main CSV's actual
source refs and ensure all prior groups/counts are retained. Use the existing

## Native and phone verification

CMake now registers 118 tests after the course-export regression; the existing
native build directory predates that extra registration. The full native build succeeded with two
pre-existing ignored QFile::open fixture warnings. The initial 116-suite run passed
115 and crashed in app_selftest because the library fixture was a read-only
symlink. Only the worktree fixture was replaced with a private APFS library clone
and SQLite backup. Original library and main files were not overwritten.

The next app run exposed a Catalog probe writing the saved real proposal path.
929f012 replaces that probe’s shared-file snapshot/overwrite with QTemporaryDir.
Independent review approved it; rebuilt app_selftest passed ALL PASS in 307.52s,
with bank through 171. Its log is `baseline/app-selftest-output-isolated-catalog.log`.
Other Preferences selftest QSettings behavior is not isolated; do not claim the
entire harness is hermetic. Do not repeat unrelated native checks without a new
change, failure, or concrete concern.

A 4,000-cloze pack built with bank through 171 had zero unplaceable answers. Signed
Xcode build and codesign verification passed after approved access to the existing
local signing/trust services; no registration or publishing flags were used.
Bundle-pack SHA-256: `ffd7ec4bafdacf1feae5d86416fa30173bb448c39d4423ade75cae0f3c187510`.
Install succeeded on device `9943CE3E-B27C-50CA-9112-C37EDA49A798`, bundle
`org.asianlegacylibrary.TibetanTrainer`. Launch failed explicitly because the
phone was locked. User was asked once to unlock; no answer yet. Do not repeat
that question or infer unlocking from elapsed time. Launch/display remain unverified.

The pack’s fixed-size sets are bounded samples: silent and peel currently contain
C01 only. Cloze 166 / particle 170 are corpus samples, not proof that the new alignment
spans reached the phone. Do not claim full C05 phone coverage, or that newer rows through 189
are in the installed 171 pack. Consumer details/logs are in `integration/`.

072a61f fixes the old invalid `terminate --bundle-identifier ... || true` call:
`ios_terminate_existing.py` queries only the installed bundle path and matches
exact executable URLs, then terminates positive PIDs via supported --pid. Query,
parse, termination and launch failures propagate. --no-launch is preserved;
normal launch also uses --terminate-existing. Six regression methods/21 scenarios,
registered CTest and both census checks passed; Windows is visibly skipped 77.
Independent source review is in `integration/deployment-review.md`. The new helper
has not been hardware-tested; no extra device actions are needed while locked.

## 2026-09-12T20:11:19.637783+00:00 — reconciliation correction1 pending

# C05:262–264 reconciliation correction 1 — consecutive members

Ruling: ALIGNMENT_LAYER_SPEC.md:78–85 says a d7 span immediately follows its d5 parent. Interpret this as a contiguous member block after that parent, with no intervening flat span; it does not limit each compound to one member. The unchanged canonical tools/gen_alignment_page.py:113–151 explicitly gathers multiple children under the same parent and reorders them within the English parent. Its resolve routine retains that parent across consecutive d7 spans. The original English263 proposal already passed this canonical behavior for both members.

Finding: Reconciled263 omitted original English w32 khrag→blood solely because rnag→pus intervenes between it and the rnag khrag→blood and pus parent. This structural explanation is false. Original exact ranges: parent Tibetan313:323/English470:483; rnag Tibetan313:317/English480:483; khrag Tibetan318:323/English470:475. Both members are tighter, unique constituents in their respective parent ranges. Their positive glossary HTG2016 records remain separate semantic evidence, not an automatic approval.
